## PR D review (savings goal, 38394fe..903c976)

### Not judged
- Visual rendering and contrast: I read the code only and did not run the app.
- Whether `pnpm check`, `db:test` and e2e pass: I took the implementer's ledger (931/931, goals pgTAP 24/24) on trust and did not re-run anything.
- Mockup fidelity: GoalCard is an interim component that PR E redraws as the Save jar's goal line.

### Strengths
- **SQL is clean.** Every function is `security definer` with `set search_path = ''`. `goal_child_family()` has its execute grant revoked from public, anon and authenticated, and the other four functions have it revoked from public and anon and granted only to `authenticated`. There is a policy per operation (insert, update and delete all deny), plus revoked table privileges.
  - Family scoping is safe: `family_members.profile_id` is unique, and the RLS select policy uses `is_parent_of`, which requires the parent and the child to be approved members of the same family.
- **Writing `reached_at` during a parent's read is acceptable.** It is idempotent (`where reached_at is null`), matches spec §5.4 ("reads compute this, and a function persists it idempotently"), and is limited to self or parent.
- **Money maths is right.** All amounts are integer minor units. `greatest(balance − reserved_save, 0)` matches the TS `goalProgress`, and that function stays the only TS place the maths lives (the Task 6 ruling to feed it SQL's counted figure with reserved 0 is sound). The paused-schedule ruling is consistent end to end: `runningSchedule` keeps paused schedules, and SQL uses `status <> 'stopped'`. JPY works through `parseMoney`, `toMajorString` and `formatMoney`, which use the currency's minor digits.
- **Review Focus 3 and 5 are covered.** `progress.test.ts` covers a target below what is saved (remaining 0, payments left 0, fill ratio 1, reached). GoalCard renders "Goal reached". pgTAP covers both plan limits, with and without a schedule.
- **Errors stay contained.** HomeGoal has its own `<Suspense>` and returns null on failure (with `unstable_rethrow`). The handover page reads the goal best-effort, so a failed read never blocks a handover.
- **Both writes go through `runOnline`,** and refresh only on `ok`.
- **The plan never touches the handover prefill,** and a test checks this. Copy uses "each time" throughout, and the archive confirm step is clear ("This goal closes. Your money stays in Save.").
- **Accessibility basics are in place:** a fieldset and legend around the radio group with a shared `name="emoji"`, 44 px (`size-11`) targets, a labelled progressbar, the live preview in `role="status"`, and the plan hint wired with `aria-describedby`.
- **Layering and barrels follow CLAUDE.md:** client barrel plus `server/index.ts` with `server-only`, `createAction` with a currency-aware schema, and DB message keys mapped only in the repository.

### Issues

**Critical:** none.

**Important:** none.

**Minor**
1. **`src/app/(app)/child/page.tsx:51-63`**: the "See your Save requests" link (`-mt-3`) was written to hug the request actions. HomeGoal now sits between them, so the link hangs under the goal card and reads as goal-related. **Fix:** mount `<HomeGoal>` after the link.
2. **`src/features/goals/components/goal-sheet.tsx:822`**: the hint "Up to {toMajorString(...)} from each allowance" shows "Up to 5.00" with no currency symbol and forced decimals. This breaks the Global Constraint that child screens use `formatMoney(..., { showMinor: "nonzero" })`. **Fix:** use `formatMoney`.
3. **`goal-sheet.tsx:788-801`**: the emoji error ("Pick a picture for your goal.") appears in `#emoji-error`, but no radio has `aria-describedby` or `aria-invalid`, so screen-reader users are not told. **Fix:** add `aria-describedby="emoji-error"` to the fieldset or radios when the error is present.
4. **`supabase/migrations/20261031023826_goals.sql:1580-1589`**: the update-then-insert in `save_goal` is not race-safe. Two first-time saves at once (two tabs, or a retry after a timeout) make one fail with `23505`, which surfaces as the generic "We couldn't save your goal." **Fix:** use `insert ... on conflict (child_id) where status = 'active' do update`, or catch `unique_violation` and retry the update. Low impact at about 60 users.
5. **`src/features/goals/components/goal-card.tsx:485`**: `Math.round(fillRatio*100)` can show 100% (and a full bar) while "£0.20 to go" is still shown. **Fix:** use `Math.floor`, or cap at 99 unless reached.
6. **Test gaps:**
   - The 22023 → plan field-error mapping in `repository.ts:1266-1271` has no unit test (the GoalSheet test mocks the action's result).
   - pgTAP checks `plan_over_target` with a null message, so a wrong key would still pass.
   - pgTAP never checks a sibling child calling `goal_progress(c1)` (should be JA403), and the archive test never asserts that g2 is `active`.
   - The reached-preview line ("You've already saved enough for this.") is untested.

   **Fix:** add these assertions.
7. **`src/app/(app)/parent/children/[childId]/handover/[kind]/[id]/page.tsx:34`**: logs the raw `{ error }`, while child Home logs `errorName(error)`. **Fix:** match child Home's form for logging hygiene.
8. **`child/page.tsx:168`**: `getAvailability` (not wrapped in `cache`) now runs twice on child Home, once in HomeRequestActions and once in HomeGoal. **Fix:** wrap it in React `cache`, or accept the extra query (cheap at this scale).
9. **`docs/fr-audit.md:12`**: FR02 still says "Not started". Plan Task 13 defers fr-audit to PR E, so this follows the plan, but the audit is stale if PR D ships alone.

### Verdict
**Ready to merge: Yes.** Security, money maths, plan alignment and error isolation are sound, and every judgement call in the ledger holds. The minors are polish; 1 and 2 are the ones a child would notice, and they are quick to fix before merge or in PR E.
